DEF:The muscle that inserts on the antenna.
ZH:触角肌
INFLECT:antennal muscles
PHONETC:/ænˈtɛnəl ˈmʌsəl/

DEF:The carina that is transverse and extends across the lateral wall of the terebra.
ZH:(产卵器侧壁横脊)
INFLECT:annuli|annular|annulate
PHONETC:/ˈænjʊləs/

NAME:anepimeron

NAME:anapleurite

INFLECT:anapleurites|anapleural
PHONETC:/ˌænəˈplʊəraɪt/

ZH:(环纹带型)|(环纹模式)
INFLECT:banding patterns
PHONETC:/ˈbændɪŋ ˈpætən/

PHONETC:/ˈbeɪsəl ɑːˌtɪkjʊˈleɪʃən/

NAME:admedian clypeal furrow

ZH:(唇基近中沟)
INFLECT:admedian clypeal furrows
PHONETC:/ædˈmiːdiən ˈklɪpiːəl ˈfʌrəʊ/

NAME:foretarsal rake

DEF:The area that is limited medially by the medial stipital wall and proximolaterally by the margin of the posterior stipital sclerite.
ZH:(茎节后壁)
INFLECT:posterior stipital walls
PHONETC:/pɒˈstɪəriər ˈstɪpɪtəl wɔːl/

NAME:posterior stipital sclerite
DEF:The sclerite that is located on the posterior stipital wall, articulates with the cardo and with the labial palp, is connected by conjunctiva distolaterally to the galeo-lacinial complex, proximally to the hypostoma and the cardo and proximolaterally to the mandible.
ZH:(茎节后骨片)
INFLECT:posterior stipital sclerites|posterior stipital sclerotized
PHONETC:/pɒˈstɪəriə ˈstɪpɪtəl ˈsklɪəraɪt/

NAME:principal carina of stipes
DEF:The flange that extends along the margin of the posterior stipital wall.
ZH:(茎节主隆线)|(茎节主脊)
INFLECT:principal carinae of stipes|principal carinal of stipes
PHONETC:/ˈprɪnsɪpəl kəˈraɪnə ɒv ˈstaɪpiːz/

NAME:maxillary palpal socket
DEF:The margin of the posterior stipital sclerite that is concave and accommodates the base of the maxillary palp.
ZH:下颚须窝|(下颚须基座)
INFLECT:maxillary palpal sockets
PHONETC:/mækˈsɪləri ˈpælpəl ˈsɒkɪt/

NAME:cardinal condyle of the stipes
DEF:The condyle that articulates with the stipital fossa of the cardo.
ZH:(茎节轴节髁)
INFLECT:cardinal condyles of the stipes|cardinal condylar of the stipes
PHONETC:/ˈkɑːdɪnəl ˈkɒndaɪl ɒv ðə ˈstaɪpiːz/

NAME:ventral dististipital process
DEF:The projection that is located distally on the posterior stipital sclerite and encircles the base of the maxillary palp.
ZH:(腹侧茎节远端突)
INFLECT:ventral dististipital processes|ventral dististipital processal
PHONETC:/ˈvɛntrəl ˌdɪstɪˈstɪpɪtəl ˈprəʊsɛs/

NAME:anterior tentorio-stipital muscle
DEF:The tentorio-stipital muscle that is anterior to the posterior tentorio-stipital muscle.
ZH:幕骨-茎节前肌
INFLECT:anterior tentorio-stipital muscles
PHONETC:/ænˈtɪəriə tɛnˈtɔːriəʊ ˈstɪpɪtl ˈmʌsl/

NAME:posterior tentorio-stipital muscle
DEF:The tentorio-stipital muscle that is posterior to the anterior tentorio-stipital muscle.
ZH:后幕骨茎节肌
INFLECT:posterior tentorio-stipital muscles
PHONETC:/pɒˈstɪəriə tɛnˈtɔːriəʊ ˈstɪpɪtəl ˈmʌsl/

NAME:cardinal fragmentum
DEF:The sclerite that is elongate, paired, is lateral to the cardo and is articulated with the cardo and with the stipes.
ZH:(轴节侧片)
INFLECT:cardinal fragmenta|cardinal fragmentum
PHONETC:/ˈkɑːdɪnəl fræɡˈmɛntəm/

NAME:medial stipital groove
DEF:The depression that extends medially and adjacent to the medial portion of the principal carina of stipes.
ZH:(茎节中沟)|(茎节内侧沟)
INFLECT:medial stipital grooves|medial stipital grooved
PHONETC:/ˈmiːdiəl ˈstɪpɪtl ɡruːv/

NAME:ocellar diameter
DEF:The diameter of the ocellus.
ZH:单眼直径
INFLECT:ocellar diameters
PHONETC:/ɒˈsɛlə daɪˈæmɪtə/

NAME:palpal formula
DEF:The ratio of the number of the maxillary sclerites and labial sclerites.
ZH:(须肢式)
INFLECT:palpal formulae|palpal formulas
PHONETC:/ˈpælpəl ˈfɔːmjʊlə/

NAME:first sclerite of the maxillary palp

ZH:(下颚须第一骨片)

PHONETC:/fɜːrst ˈsklɪəraɪt ɒv ðə mækˈsɪləri pælp/
